## Document 013: 20260910-AAPL新品發表會

### Page 1

Apple
秋季新品發表會
2026.09

### Page 2

iPhone 18 Pro與iPhone 18 Pro Max推出
• 蘋果新一代高階旗艦智慧型手機 ，在智慧功能、處理效能、電池續航與攝影系統四方面迎來
重大突破。外觀及材質採用同色調配色的鋁金屬邊框結合一體成型背面玻璃 ，提供4種配色，
包括太空黑、銀色、冰川藍以及勃根地紅。
• 螢幕互動介面：動態島縮小，支援同時顯示多達 3 個即時動態。
• Siri AI整合：由最新的Apple Foundation Models與端側模型驅動，具備擬真人聲、自訂語
速抑揚頓挫、螢幕感知、跨App執行指令與全系統精確聽寫。
• iPhone接力通話：允許兩部 iPhone共享同一個電話號碼 ，拿起任一部手機即能無縫通話
（首波與T-Mobile、德國電信合作，後續納入Verizon與EE）。
• AirDrop速度提升達 80%，行動網路與 Wi-Fi 自動無縫切換。
1
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理

### Page 3

18 pro系列核心處理器升級：A20 Pro 晶片
• 先進製程：採用最新2nm製程技術打造，具備更高的電晶體密度。
• 6核心CPU：配置2顆桌面級全新超級核心，效能提升達20%，為智慧型手機中最快核心；搭配4顆
高能效核心，並全數整合神經加速器以驅動低延遲裝置端AI。
• 7核心GPU：圖形運算效能較前代大幅躍升 40%，微架構針對3A級手遊優化；配備全新神經加速
器，其8位元浮點運算吞吐量提升達2倍，可於本機直接運行第三方大型語言模型。
• 雙神經網路引擎：額外配置第二組神經網路引擎，核心總數達32核心，本機AI運算能力倍增。
• 記憶體介面：記憶體頻寬大幅提升50%，為 iPhone 歷來最寬的記憶體匯流排介面。
• WMCM封裝：借鑒Apple M系列晶片架構，採用全新特製封裝，將晶圓裸晶與記憶體並排佈局，
移出SoC主要熱路徑，使晶粒直接與均熱板貼合，提升散熱效能。
2
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理

### Page 4

散熱系統與續航快充
• 新一代散熱Vapor Chamber：表面積為iPhone 17 Pro的3倍，內部去離子水持續循環導熱；
均熱板採用80%回收不銹鋼製成，並搭配高導熱石墨片。
• 奈米雙晶銅材料：業界首創散熱材料 ，能增強機身結構剛性，並快速將熱量自顯示螢幕引導
排除。
• 持續效能釋放：高負載下持續效能較iPhone 17 Pro提升 40%，為iPhone 16 Pro及更早機
型的2倍。
• 電池續航力：(1)iPhone 18 Pro：機身小巧，但擁有與iPhone 17 Pro Max相同的全天候續
航；純 eSIM 機型之影片播放時間達 36 小時；真實綜合日常使用模型下可達 24小時。(2)
iPhone 18 Pro Max純eSIM機型影片播放時間高達 45 小時；綜合使用模型下每次充電可達
30 小時。有線快速充電：充電約 15 分鐘可充至 50% 電量；充電 5 分鐘可提供約 6 小時影
片播放時間。
3
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理

### Page 5

專業相機系統
• 4,800萬像素Fusion主相機：搭載可變光圈 ，採用6片雷射切割葉片 ，葉片厚度薄於人類頭髮 ，由特製聚
合物複合材料製成；搭配微型轉子機構與客製光學鏡片組。
• 可調節光圈：暗光環境自動切換至 F1.8 大光圈，進光量增加約 50%，提升夜間人像與星空攝影品質；光
線充足時縮小光圈以加深景深，確保群體合照邊角與前後背景清晰。
• Pro Controls：提供相機手動操作介面，支援手動微調4檔光圈、白平衡、直方圖曝光監控、手動快門速
度調整。
• 錄影與對焦升級：支援拍攝後添加電影級景深且開放60 FPS半速慢動作；支援4K Dolby Vision HDR縮時
攝影；Audio Mix升級聲音與音樂演算法；智慧對焦追蹤利用AI本機模型牢牢鎖定進出畫面的動態主體。
• 攝影風格升級：新增照片紋理與膠卷顆粒感調節，可即時調整膚質質感並支援無損復原。
• 影像真實性：除相容SynthID標記AI生成圖外，推出Apple Reference Image憑證機制。感光元件在拍攝

瞬間即在像素層級對照片進行安全密碼學簽署 ，私有雲端運算會沖印成一份不可修改的數位負片 ，與一
般可編輯相片共同儲存於照片App中，以證明照片原始未經變造。
4
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理

### Page 6

蘋果首款摺疊旗艦：iPhone Duo
• 蘋果推出首款旗艦摺疊機iPhone Duo，將iPad級別的大螢幕整合至小巧機身中，採書本式對折，非對稱
式對角圓角線條，展開時為iPhone歷來最薄機身，核心組件高度整合在相機凸起處內部 ，留出內部空間
配置雙電池模組。螢幕正面為Ceramic Shield 2、背面為Ceramic Shield，並具備完整IP68等級防塵防水。
• 機身：邊框由5級鈦金屬精工切削並呈現拋光鏡面質感；天線斷點處導入 陶瓷纖維增強嵌件強度為5級鈦
金屬的3倍。
• 內外螢幕：展開為7.6 吋Super Retina XDR螢幕，具窄邊框與一致的長寬比，外螢幕：5.4 吋顯示螢幕，
具備相當於iPhone 18 Pro 90%的可用顯示面積。內螢幕影片可播放31小時，外螢幕播放達44小時，雙
螢幕平均綜合使用續航達24小時。
• 後置鏡頭：4,800萬像素 Fusion 主鏡頭(支援2x光學品質望遠、4K 120 FPS慢動作錄影與4K杜比視界縮
時)+4,800 萬像素 Fusion超廣角鏡頭。

• Apple Pencil支援：雙螢幕皆支援Apple Pencil(USB-C)，為iPhone首度支援Apple Pencil手寫與繪圖。
• 側邊Touch ID：於側邊電源鍵整合TouchID指紋辨識(可註冊多組指紋)，亦支援Apple Watch自動解鎖。
5
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理

### Page 7

iPhone Duo雙晶片架構，A20 Pro及自研C2晶片
6
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理
• A20 Pro晶片：採用2奈米與裸晶直貼散熱封裝 ，配合大型均熱板，持續效能較 iPhone 17
Pro提升35%；內建全新顯示引擎 ，可同時驅動雙螢幕並消除螢幕下鏡頭邊緣像素過渡走樣 。
• Apple C2晶片：深度整合基頻、RF收發器、PMIC與通訊韌體，上傳速率較上一代C1X提升
達50%，功耗降低15%。在美國市場正式加入5G mmWave頻段支援。
• 全eSIM架構與雙電池：全球市場全面採純 eSIM配置；機身兩側各配置一組高能量密度電池 ，
由客製晶片與演算法自動調配充放平衡。

### Page 8

iPhone Duo無摺痕螢幕技術
• 鉸鏈：由超過100枚精密微型零組件與碳纖維承載支撐板組成 ，可變扭力曲線讓開闔阻尼手
感順暢，並整合磁鐵陣列牢固吸合。
• 螢幕防護多層貼合結構：表面採用奈米紋理抗眩光表面 ，利用無光罩雷射微影技術逐像素雕
刻微透鏡，呈現視覺平整霧面感，使中央摺痕在視覺上近乎隱形 。表層覆蓋高抗刮客製聚合
物，剛性提升40%。OLED面板上下皆夾有高強度超薄玻璃 ，並灌注黏彈性膠材 ，摺疊時各
層能如書頁滑移，避免反覆摺疊疲勞形變；底層襯有鈦金屬板強化支撐。
• 雷射製造公差控制：組裝以AI演算法將鉸鏈與外殼進行公差吻合配對；出廠前以共軛焦雷射
逐台掃描表面拓撲，並3D列印沉積多達25層光固化聚合物微層，消除微波紋。
7
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理

### Page 10

新一代無線藍芽耳機AirPods 5
• 全新第5代半開放式真無線耳機，正式在全線AirPods系列中全面普及主動式降噪，降噪效能
比前代提升 50%。延續半開放式配戴設計，重新設計內部構造，貼合各類耳形幾何結構。
• 多孔聲學架構：全新多孔內部聲學管道 ，搭配新一代適應性等化功能 ，大幅提升低頻飽滿度
與高頻細節。
• 個人化空間音訊：提供包圍感更強的動態頭部追蹤空間音訊體驗。
• 通透模式與適應性音訊通透模式更加自然；適應性音訊能根據周圍環境噪音強度 ，在主動降
噪與通透模式之間即時自動混合調配。
• Apple Intelligence與Siri AI整合：可免持利用語音調用Siri AI，結合個人脈絡資料解答疑問；
支援即時翻譯功能。
9
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理

### Page 11

Apple Watch Series 12/Apple Watch Ultra 4
• Apple Watch Series 12搭載新一代 S11 晶片，首度採二代Ceramic Shield 2，螢幕耐刮耐摔強度比前代
Ion-X玻璃高 60%。
• 電極接觸面積擴增：在相同錶背面積下增加電極表面積，提升ECG心電圖偵測的皮膚導電與接觸精準度。
• 環狀光電二極體陣列：重新設計的光電二極體以環狀結構排列，大幅提升對反射光線的捕捉效率。
• 全新健康App功能：(1)由 Apple Intelligence 自動生成個人生理趨勢摘要(如睡眠走勢或 VO2 Max 峰值)，
並動態給出間歇跑等運動調整建議 。(2)將體能指標與血檢生物標記對比實際年齡 ，分析日常習慣之健康
影響。(3)全方位量化睡眠、活動與心臟表現，支援手動同步病歷，或以 119 美元向Quest訂購50項生物
標記的血檢套裝，並在本機結合專家影片給出醫學解讀。(4)搭配iPhone相機與Apple Watch感測器，即

時語音校正關節動作，輔助 VO2 Max 測試或運動傷害復健進度追蹤。
• Apple Watch Ultra 4：為極限運動、專業冒險家與耐力賽事選手設計之頂級智慧手錶，日常一般使用續
航時間達2天以上。全功能開啟狀態(完整心率追蹤+全頻雙頻GPS)下，體能訓練時間達18小時。
10
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理

### Page 12

相關台股供應鏈
• 全新(2455)、穩懋(3105)：因Wi-Fi 7相較於Wi-Fi 6在手機中新增6GHz的頻段，同時由於對PA性能要求，
將轉向使用GaAs作為材料。我們認為相關供應鏈如全新(2455)、穩懋(3105)，將受惠於手機廠導入Wi-Fi 7
，下半年PA相關台股供應鏈營運展望正向，若後續iPhone新機銷售表現佳，有望進一步對獲利有所挹注。
• 大立光(3008)：大立光受惠iPhone 18新機拉貨動能，且今年新旗艦機首度搭載可變光圈鏡頭，並且為主要
供應商，為2H26營運增加動能。大立光切入CPO應用，有利於推升評價，雖短期營收能見度有限，但預估
將於2028年實現量產並貢獻營收，有望成為自手機鏡頭外公司第二成長動能。過往大立光在手機鏡頭本業
的營運上升週期下 ，預估本益比約交易在 15至18倍左右，而公司成功切入CPO元件市場，市場現給予25-
30倍以上本益比左右。
• 奇鋐(3017)：過去iPhone 17 Pro 首度導入超薄 VC，隨AI 負載提升，我們預期 VC 將逐步成為蘋果高階手

機、平板及折疊旗艦標配。折疊機預計採用雙片式超薄VC，帶動單機用量與ASP倍增；我們預期奇鋐可取
得約50%市占率，有望明顯受惠於蘋果高階手持裝置散熱需求成長。
• 新日興(3376)：在折疊機軸承(Hinge)方面，Apple為實現極致的無感摺痕與耐用度 ，軸承內部結構更為複
雜，零件數及加工精度均較現有非蘋產品大幅提升，帶動單機軸承模組 ASP 接近倍增。台系供應鏈中，我
們認為新日興(3376)受惠程度最高。新日興與主要組裝代工廠鴻海(2317) 透過合資關係合作，有助於整合
機構件製造與組裝，並提升初期量產良率；我們預期其有望取得 Apple 首款折疊機的高階軸承訂單，成為
新日興中長期獲利的重要成長動能。
• 鴻海(2317): 鴻海負責蘋果首款摺疊機的整機組裝與最終調整，並與軸承廠新日興深化合作，共同搶攻高精
密轉軸商機。高毛利的新機效應將顯著拉抬整體產品組合與獲利表現。結合 AI 伺服器業務的持續高成長，
未來營運展望樂觀。
11資料來源：中信投顧整理

HDI concerns easing
New capacity and price hikes boosting earnings visibility for 2027-28
We are increasingly confident in Gold Circuit’s transition towards higher-layer-
count HDI boards, supported by meaningful investments in technology, talent, and
new capacity. The planned capacity ramp aligns well with the next-generation

ASIC schedules of two major CSP customers, while potential layer-count increase
from 26 layers for Trainium3 to 34 layers for Trainium4 should drive meaningful
content growth. Meanwhile, PCB price hikes and improving cost pass-through
should support a sequential GM recovery from 3Q26. We raise our 2027/28 EPS
forecasts by 1%/9% and lift our TP from NT$1,955 to NT$2,000 while reiterating
our High Conviction Outperform rating.
Less concerned about the HDI transition

While the market remains concerned about Gold Circuit’s HDI capabilities, we
believe the company has recruited experienced talent and invested meaningfully in
HDI technology to support future ASIC demand. The second Thailand plant and
new capacity at the first Suzhou plant are scheduled to contribute from 2H27,
followed by the second Suzhou plant in 1H28, with all three expansions primarily
dedicated to HDI production. Importantly, the capacity ramp should align well with

the next-generation ASIC schedules of two major CSP customers. We expect Gold
Circuit to maintain its leading position in AWS Trainium4 and benefit from
meaningful content growth, driven by an increase in PCB layer count from 26 layers
for Trainium3 to 34 layers for Trainium4.
Price hikes underway
For AI accelerator PCBs, we believe Gold Circuit should gradually pass rising CCL
costs through to customers. Our checks suggest that non-AI PCB products are

being quoted at 3-4x prior price levels, with lead-times extending to at least four
months at leading PCB vendors. This indicates a tightening industry supply-demand
environment and suggests that the GM weakness seen in 2Q26 should not persist
in the coming quarters. We forecast GM to improve sequentially to 36.7%/36.8%
in 3Q26/4Q26.
Earnings revisions and valuation
We keep 2026SP EPS largely unchanged but raise our 2027/28 forecasts by 1%/9%
to reflect higher production value contributed by the new plants and increasing HDI

content. Accordingly, we raise our TP from NT$1,955 to NT$2,000, still based on
30x 2H27-1H28SP PE, and reiterate our High Conviction Outperform rating.

### Page 5

HDI concerns easing Gold Circuit - HC O-PF
14 September 2026 5
Investment thesis
We expect Gold Circuit to benefit from the strong CCL/PCB material upgrade trend,
rising shipments of AI servers and continued spec upgrades in CPU servers, which
require higher PCB layer counts. The upgrade in networking towards 800G should
also lead to improving margins for the company.
Catalysts
1) Accelerating server shipments driven by US CSPs and enterprise customers; 2)

server content value growth; 3) a stronger-than-expected networking demand
recovery; 4) a notebook demand recovery.
Valuation details
We value all of our coverage in the PCB/CCL space on PE as it better reflects
margins and earnings outlooks, which we believe the market will mainly focus on.
We use a target PE multiple of 30x, in line with the downstream server sector
average after the recent rerating driven by AI. Given GCE's higher-than-peer
revenue exposure to cloud servers and switches, its leading market position, and

our expectation of robust AI/ASIC server shipments with computing server demand
to gradually recover, we think 30x PE is justified as server/PC ODM suppliers have
all rerated to the 15-30x range on the back of the booming AI demand. And as GCE
also generates a similar number of sales from server and AI, it will likely enjoy strong
growth (better content growth from server and switch) and better returns (less
competition) than assemblers in the mid-to-long term.

Investment risks
Downside risks: (1) order adjustment as a result of chip shortages or supply chain
disruption; (2) weaker-than-expected cloud/datacentre Capex demand; (3) slower
adoption or delayed launch of Intel’s Eagle Stream and AMD’s Genoa platforms; (4)
further raw material price hikes, and (5) more severe competition across the
industry.

營收走勢圖

電機機械  
上銀(2049.TT) 
AI 與半導體需求強勁，精密線性傳動元件交期持續拉長

AI 與半導體需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環，上銀生產
的精密線性傳動元件大量用於 AI 與半導體設備 ， 在半導體產業CapEx
確立的情況下，預期這波拉貨潮將延續至明年。在上一波 2021-22 年
的上升循環中，股價最高來到當年度 EPS 的 45 倍，考量到目前僅出
現第一波漲價，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 13.16 元給予 35x 本益比，目
標價為 460 元，給予買進的評等。

1. 半導體設備需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環 
上銀生產的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿大量應用於 AI 與半導體設備當中，這
波景氣上升循環由從 4Q25 開始，拉貨動能逐季提升。從交期來看，線
性滑軌的交期從去年年底 3 個月，拉長到現在的 4~5 個月；滾珠螺桿
交期從去年年底 4 個月，拉長到現在 5~6 個月。從營收來看，扣除 2
月份有過年因素外 (去年過年在 1 月)，上銀的月營收 YoY 從 3 月的
17.4%一路提升到 8 月的 42.3%，此期間沒有縮小過，顯示營收不但在
成長，成長的動能還在加速。公司亦提到從 6 月開始 BB raio 大於 1，
需求非常強勁，已開始規劃明年過年期間要如何加班生產。研究部看好
這波由 AI 與半導體帶動的工具機需求延續至明年，預估上銀 2026/27
年營收為 300.1/378.2 億元，+23.7%/+26.0% YoY。

2. 市場供不應求，交期持續拉長看好後續漲價機會 
市場供不應求 ， 公司於今年3 月宣布調漲 7~15%， 已於6 月開始反應 。
2Q26 毛利率 34.2%，季增 2.2 個百分點，為稼動率與價格同步提升所
致，預估 3Q26 漲價效應完全反應後毛利率將進一步提升至 35.2%，研
究部預估上銀 2026/27 年的毛利率為 34.3%/35.6%， 分別年增5.5/1.3
個百分點，EPS 為 9.56/13.16 元，+121.7%/+37.6% YoY。 
這波由半導體產業 CapEx 所帶動的景氣上升，研究部看好在交期不斷拉長
的情況下，後續還有漲價、上修毛利率的機會。(上一波 2021-2022 年上升
循環中毛利率最高來到 37-38%)

一、 一、公司介紹 
   上銀科技成立於 1989 年 ， 專注於線性滑軌 、 滾珠螺桿與單軸機器人等精密線性
傳動元件的製造 ， 為該領域的全球領導廠商之一 。 除了持續研發線性傳動產品以外 ，
也自行開發出多種半導體自動化設備 ，產品廣泛應用於汽車 、 半導體 、 醫療 、 國防 、
航太及食品等產業。上銀憑藉自有品牌 「HIWIN」 的深厚研發實力與品質行銷全世
界，目前為全球第二大生產線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿的廠商，第一大為日商 THK 
(6481.T)。1H26 產品營收占比﹔線性滑軌 60%、 滾珠螺桿20%、 工業機器人12%、
其他 8%。2025 年地區營收占比﹔台灣 44%、中國 21%、德國 16%、美國 5%、
日本 4%、其他 10%。(地區營收 2025 年已是最新公布)

二、 產業分析 
上銀主要產品為線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿，產品功能如下﹔ 
1. 線性滑軌﹔一種線性傳動元件，由滑塊與滑軌組成，透過內部的滾珠或滾  
柱循環系統來降低摩擦，讓平台保持平行穩定的直線運動。  
2. 滾珠螺桿﹔一種線性傳動元件，由螺桿、螺帽與鋼珠組成，利用鋼珠在螺桿與
螺帽間循環滾動，將旋轉運動轉換為直線運動。

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 4

圖四、滾珠螺桿

商業模式 
線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿為工具機零組件 ， 工具機產業商業模式分為以下三種 (以 AOI
檢測設備為例)﹔ 
1. 工具機零組件廠生產線性滑軌 ➨ 工具機 OEM 廠將各零組件組裝成五軸加工
機 ➨ 設備零組件廠用五軸加工機生產高精度定位平台  ➨ 設備商用高精度定位
平台與其他零組件組裝成 AOI 檢測設備。  
2. 工具機零組件廠生產線性滑軌 ➨ 工具機 OEM 廠將各零組件組裝成五軸加工
機 ➨ 設備商用五軸加工機自行生產所需的高精度定位平台或其他零組件。 
3. 工具機零組件廠生產的線性滑軌同時是工具機零組件與設備零組件  ➨ 設備
商直接購買線性滑軌來組裝。

圖五、商業模式

資料來源：統一投顧整理

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 5

產業趨勢 
JMTBA (日本工具機協會) 每月會將日本所有工具機 OEM 廠的訂單加總 ， 公布 「日
本工具機訂單金額」 ，其產品包含多軸加工機、CNC 車床、滾齒機……等等，由於
日本擁有全球規模前幾大的工具機 OEM 廠，如 DMG、Yamazaki、Okuma，且多
年來產品遍布各個產業所累積的經驗也使得這些公司不論在自研控制器的演算
法，還是機器長時間運轉下維持的精度與溫度都與其他國家有技術上的差距。  
因此，在各行各業景氣轉好，需要大量的工具機時，JMTBA 公布的訂單金額被視
為是工具機產業最重要的觀察指標，工具機 OEM 廠訂單增加 ➨ 增加向工具機
零組件廠的拉貨。 
觀察過去十年日本工具機訂單的走勢，可發現景氣大約呈現兩年上升、兩年下降
的循環。上一次的上升週期為 2021-22 年疫情加上塞港，全球各產業瘋狂拉貨，
使得工具機訂單 YoY 從 2020/11 的-6%，最高拉升到 2021/5 的 142%，一直到
2022/10 訂單 YoY 才轉負 。 塞港造成的恐慌式補庫存為工具機產業帶來20 年來不
曾有過的成長率 ， 也使得隨後各產業去化庫存時 ， 工具機這次的景氣休息得較久 ，
直到 2H25 才再次迎來上升週期。

圖六、日本工具機訂單及 YoY

資料來源：JMTBA

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 6

圖七、上銀過去十年股價走勢圖

資料來源：Wind 
（註：與日本工具機訂單 YoY 高度聯動）

而在此波的上升週期，生產線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿等工具機零組件全球市占前二的
廠商，THK、上銀一致提到這一次的需求來自 AI 與半導體，且需求非常強勁。 
細看 AI 與半導體產業，其中涵蓋了相當多工具機的蹤跡，舉凡伺服器散熱所需的
水冷板、快接頭及熱交換器，到伺服器機殼、Compute/GPU Tray 與各種結構件，
都需要以工具機進行切削或沖壓。半導體產業中，在製程升級與產能擴充的過程
需要大量的半導體設備 ，不論在前 、 中 、 後段的設備都須使用線性傳動元件 ， 如蝕
刻機、AOI 檢測設備、各類型 Bonder 等等。 
產品規格方面，AI 與半導體所需的工具機精度更高，使得 THK、上銀這種技術領
先的廠商進一步受惠 。 一般工業用工具機僅需使用H/N 級的線性滑軌與 C5/C7 級
的滾珠螺桿，生產水冷板與快接頭時需使用 SP/P 級的線性滑軌與 C3 級的滾珠螺
桿；在Die Bonder (晶粒鍵合機)與 Flip Chip Bonder (倒裝鍵合機)上則需使用 UP/SP
級的線性滑軌與 C0/C1 級的滾珠螺桿，生產高精度的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿毛利率
顯著高於精度較低的。

人) 、Wafer Aligner (晶圓尋邊器)，目前認列於機器人營收當中，產能滿載，直接
出貨給半導體製程廠商。

在 AI 的快速發展下，半導體製程升級與產能擴張帶動半導體資本支出持續增加，

小結 
歷經 2022 年後的全球各產業庫存去化，工具機景氣委靡了接近三年的期間，如
今挾帶著 AI 伺服器與半導體設備需求強勁成長，再次迎來新一波景氣上升循環。
從水冷板、快接頭到各類半導體設備，皆帶動工具機需求快速升溫， 相關廠商也
同步指出需求強勁、訂單持續增加，交期明顯拉長。 
更重要的是，這波需求主要來自半導體設備等高精度應用領域，所需線性滑軌與
滾珠螺桿規格高於一般工業產品，上銀除受惠於工具機景氣重新向上外，亦可進
一步受惠於高規格精密線性傳動元件需求成長。

### Page 9

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 9

三、 三、營運狀況 
1. 1. 半導體設備需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環 
上銀生產的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿大量應用於半導體設備當中，這波景氣上升循
環由從 4Q25 開始，拉貨動能逐季提升。從交期來看，線性滑軌的交期從去年
年底 3 個月 ，到五月法說會提到 3.5 個月 ， 再到八月份交期已經拉長到 5 個月 ；
滾珠螺桿交期從去年年底 4 個月，到五月法說會的 4.5 個月，再到現在的 6 個
月。從營收來看，扣除 2 月份有過年因素外 (去年過年在 1 月)，上銀的月營收
YoY 從 3 月的 17.4%一路提升到 8 月的 42.3%，此期間沒有縮小過，顯示營收
不但在成長，成長的動能還在加速。公司亦提到從6 月開始 BB raio 大於 1，需
求非常強勁，已開始規劃明 年過年期間要如何加班生產。研究部看好這波半導
體帶動的工具機需求延續至明年 ，預估上銀 2026/27 年營收為 300.1/378.2 億
元，+23.7%/+26.0% YoY。

圖十二、上銀各產品交期

資料來源：統一投顧整理

2. 2. 市場供不應求，交期持續拉長看好後續漲價機會 
市場供不應求，公司於今年 3 月宣布調漲 7~15%，已於 6 月開始反應。2Q26 毛
利率 34.2%，季增 2.2 個百分點，為稼動率與價格同步提升所致，預估 3Q26 漲價
效應完全反應後毛利率將進一步提升至 35.2%，研究部預估上銀 2026/27 年的毛
利率為 34.3%/35.6%，分別年增 5.5/1.3 個百分點 ，EPS 為 9.56/13.16 元，
+121.7%/+37.6% YoY。這波由半導體產業 CapEx 所帶動的景氣上升，研究部看
好在交期不斷拉長的情況下，後續還有漲價、上修毛利率的機會。 (上一波 2021-
2022 年上升循環中毛利率由 31%迅速拉升至 37-38%)

3. 預期景氣將延續至明年，目前評價偏低 
AI 與半導體需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環，上銀生產的精密線性傳動
元件大量用於 AI 與半導體設備，在半導體產業 CapEx 確立的情況下，預期這波
拉貨潮將延續至明年。在上一波 2021-22 年的上升循環中，股價最高來到當年度
EPS 的 45 倍，考量到目前僅出現第一波漲價，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 13.16 元給
予 35x 本益比，目標價為 460 元，給予買進的評等。

強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25 %以上

確 性 作 出 任 何 陳 述 或 保 證。本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知。
本 投 資 報 告 並 非（ 且 不 應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經

1 群益投顧 
市 場 流 傳 一 份 疑 似 台 達 電(2308 TT) 發 給 客 戶 的 通 知 指 出，特 定 批 次 電 源 供 應 器 存 在 潛
在 品 質 問 題 。 此 次 問 題 主 要 來 自 供 應 商 零 組 件 ， 並 非 台 達 電 產 品 設 計 ， 且 台 達 電 擁 有
不 同 電 容 器 供 應 商 及 備 料 ， 預 期 可 透 過 更 換 電 容 器 供 應 來 源 解 決， 影 響 有 限。 台達電
為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商 ， 受 惠 於AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動 AC/DC 電 源 大 幅
成長外， CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC 電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成
長動能 ， 投 資 評 等 為 Buy ，目標價為 2,050 元。

研 究 員 ： 王 美 珍   
iris.wang@capital.com.tw

電 容 器 更 換 事 件 對 營 運 影 響 有 限：  
市場流傳一份疑似台達 電 發給客戶的通知指出，特定批次電源供應器存在
潛 在 品 質 問 題 。內 容 表 示 根 據 調 查 ， 受 影 響 PSU 所 使 用 的 部 分 電 容 器 ， 在
未 施 加 電 壓、長 時 間 存 放 後，可 能 出 現 漏 電 流 增 加 情 況，特 定 條 件 下 恐 進 一
步 造 成 電 容 器 電 壓 不 平 衡 及 過 熱，重 新 上 電 時 可 能 導 致 電 容 器 故 障。此 次 涉
及台達 電 新一代 VR 平台 18.3 kW PSU ， 相 關 問 題 於 試 產 階 段 發 現 ， 台 達電
隨 後 通 知 客 戶 並 進 行 電 容 器 更 換 。 
台達 電 表 示 不 評 論 市 場 傳 聞；若 相 關 情 況 確 認 屬 供 應 商 零 組 件 問 題，公 司 將
依 實 際 狀 況 更 換 供 應 商 ， 並 配 合 客 戶 需 求 進 行 後 續 處 理 。 
此次 問 題 主 要 來 自 供 應 商 零 組 件，並 非 台 達 電 產 品 設 計，且 台 達電 擁 有 不 同
電 容 器 供 應 商 及 備 料 ， 預 期 可 透 過 更 換 電 容 器 供 應 來 源 解 決 。 
投 資 建 議 ：  
台達電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商， 受 惠 於 AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動
AC/DC 電 源 大 幅 成 長 外 ，CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC
電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成 長 動 能 。受 惠 於 AI 、 Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長 ，
營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 ， 投 資 評 等 為Buy ， 目 標 價 為 2, 050 元。

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

產品組合

股價相對大盤走勢

全新 (2455 TT)  Buy

2H26 、 2027 年營收動能可期，評等為 Buy。  
投資建議 ：07/2026 全新營收 4.22 億元， MoM+29.48% ，創歷史
新高，主要係 AI data center 相關需求持續熱絡，且出貨表現亦
隨磷化銦基板料況改善而回歸常軌所致，且 2H26、2027 年營收
動能仍可期，故群益對全新的投資建議為 Buy 。  
07/2026 營收創新高： 2Q26 營收 9.65 億元， QoQ+0.63% ，其中，
微電子營收 7.04 億元， QoQ-1.89% ，與 1Q26 相去有限；光電子
產品營收 2.61 億元， QoQ+8.12% ，但 01~06/2026 僅 02/2026 受
農曆春節影響而僅 0.68 億元，其餘各月份光電子營收均為
0.86~0.88 億元，換言之，缺料問題仍影響光電子產品出貨表現。  
不過， 07/2026 營收 4.22 億元， MoM+29.48% ，創歷史新高，關
鍵在於磷化銦基板缺料狀況將隨中國政府 05/2026 、 07/2026 兩
度放行管制而改善 ， 帶動全新光電子產品營收增加至 1.69 億元 ，
MoM+93.74% ；至於微電子產品營收 2.53 億元，亦為近幾年相
對高水準。  
2H26 展望優於 1H26：雖然 AI data center 相關需求熱絡，然而，
01~06/2026 光電子產品月營收成長性仍受制於磷化銦基板缺
料。全新磷化銦基板過去多數由美國業者的中國廠供應，然而，
受到中國政府管制磷化銦基板出口數量影響， 4Q25 全新光電子
產品營收逐月減少，且全新來自該供應商的磷化銦基板庫存
12/2025 已陸續用罄， 01~06/2026 磷化銦基板主要由第 2、 3 供
應商供應。  
所幸，中國政府 05/2026 月底批准 2026 年第 1 批磷化銦基板出
口， 07/2026 則再通過第 2 批，且總數量大於第 1 批，第 1、 2

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就企業溫室氣體排放量減量目標而言，由於全新專注於製造、銷售及研發化
合物半導體磊晶片，溫室氣體排放量多寡，主要和能源耗用、產能及產品組
成有關。全新乃就單位產品的能源耗用與溫室氣體排放量，設定目標為每年
減少 5%，並將視產能擴充與產品變化情形，賡續追蹤與調整設定與目標。
至於企業產品或服務帶給客戶或消費者之減碳效果的部份，全新為客戶代
工生產的產品多數運用於通訊等電子產品，非屬終端消費性產品；全新除協
助客戶設計低耗能、高效率產品，以降低產品生命週期內的資源耗用外，亦
持續進行生產製造過程的節能與減廢，以降低對環境所造成的衝擊，減少單
位產品所消耗的資源與污染物，為客戶及社會創造減碳環保效益。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

03 September 2026 Citi Research
2
Unimicron
Valuation
Our target price for Unimicron is NT$1,500. We use a 1-year forward-looking DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect
Unimicron's ability to generate stable cash. With a risk-free rate of 4.3% (10-year government bond rate), a market risk
premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 1.43 (2-year weekly share price), we calculate Unimicron's WACC as 13.7%. Our target
price is equivalent to 66x/31x 2026/27E EPS, which is higher than the +1std level of consensus PE multiple of the past five

years. However, as the market focuses on 2027 upcycle, we believe valuation on 2027E EPS is still in line with historical peak
valuations of 25–30x during the last upcycles.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean Unimicron shares trade below our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected HDI
business; 2) significant increase in ABF supply in the industry; and 3) stronger-than-expected margin pressure from
depreciation due to higher capex investments.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

## Document 071: 260903_daiwa_ Laiyi

Source PDF: all report pdf/260903_daiwa_ Laiyi.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-03
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: We received an update from Lai Yih’s management after its 
upbeat 2Q26 results (see memo, 25 August). We are more confident in its 
revenue and operating profit returning to growth YoY in 2H26E and 2027E 
on new model launches from its key clients, a new client win and better 
seasonality approaching in 4Q26E, and thus, upgrade Lai Yih to Buy (1) 
from Hold (3).

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results key highlight: better-than-expected 
client and product mix. In 2Q26, we noticed that Lai Yih’s revenue and 
gross margin beat expectations on more favourable client and product mix. 
Its operating profit of TWD1.203bn (+566.6% QoQ and +8.7% YoY) beat 
our and Bloomberg consensus estimates by a respective 26.6% and 
41.1%. Lai Yih’s client mix for 2Q26 included Adidas (53.4%; -2.5% YoY), 
Hoka (36%; +9.0% YoY, record-high revenue mix level), Converse (7.8%; -
41.5% YoY) and New Balance (2.8%; -19.6% YoY).

Brighter 2H26E and 2027E outlook with rising new model launches 
from existing clients; we assume one new client in 4Q26. We believe 
3Q26E will be off-peak season for Lai Yih, but expect its revenue 
momentum to pick up QoQ in 4Q26E, along with gross margin expansion 
QoQ. Given the company’s track record with Adidas and Hoka, 
management noted it is in discussion with several potential new clients; we 
assume one new client to onboard in 4Q26. Lai Yih aims for 2027 
shipments to return to 2025 levels. Despite continuing inflation, the 
company targets to gain more high-end shoes with higher ASP to drive its 
overall ASP and gross margin expansion. Besides, Hoka will start to initiate 
new products in 2H26, which we believe will be positive to Lai Yih’s gross 
margin. Our 2026-27E EPS remain almost unchanged, and we introduce 
our 2028 forecasts, calling for EPS growth of 10% YoY.

What we recommend: We upgrade Lai Yih to Buy (1) from Hold (3) and 
raise our 12M TP to TWD230 (from TWD160) mainly on improving 
fundamentals expected from 2H26E. Due to a recovering earnings 
trajectory, we now use a target PER of 18x (from 15x), vs. its past-3-year 
range of 12-33x (average PER of 20x), applied to our 1-year forward EPS. 
We forecast an earnings CAGR of 18.5% over 2026-28 (previously -1.6% 
over 2025-27E). Downside risks: weaker-than-expected end demand; 
lower-than-expected market share gain; inflation.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 15-22% below the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely as we are more conservative on revenue and gross 
margin. However, we believe we are more positive on Lai Yih’s client win 
capability riding on the product mix optimisation trend.

3 September 2026

Upgrading: improving fundamentals

➢ 2Q26 results beat expectations in revenue and gross margin 
➢ Brighter order visibility in 2H26E and 2027E; new client in 2H26 
➢ Upgrading to Buy (1) from Hold (3); raising 12M TP to TWD230

Company profile 
Established in 1987, Lai Yih is a footwear manufacturer that collaborates with Adidas (56.4% of total 
revenue in 2025), Hoka (28.5%), Converse (12.4%) and Uniqlo. Its expertise lies in the production of 
high-quality vulcanised shoes, athletic footwear, cold pressed shoes, and specialised functional 
footwear. Lai Yih Group is headquartered in Taichung (Taiwan) and its production plants and 
development centres for clients are mainly based in Vietnam (91% of total shipment in 2025), Myanmar 
(7%) and Indonesia (1%).

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
The CEO and chairperson of Lai Yih is not the same individual. We note that the CEO, Mr. Lin,
has 35 years of experience in this industry, which is a positive sign from an executive
perspective.
Capital management 1 Lai Yih's dividend payout ratio was 44-70% over 2022-25, which we see as rational due to its
capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 1 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was less than 1% of total revenue during
2022-25, and we note no suspicious activity.
G Supply chain
management 2
In supply chain management, the company emphasises product quality and safety. Due to its
focus on supply chain transparency, it has designated sales specialists to provide suppliers with
prompt responses to issues.
E Materials sourcing &
efficiency 2
For raw materials and energy usage, the company prioritises environmental sustainability. Its
production processes adhere to safety certifications and standardized procedures, while it strives
to improve energy efficiency and reduce pollution.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): Jul rev in line at +54% YoY; AI server and smartphones to support 3Q 
growth

Hon Hai’s Jul revenues were +54% YoY/ +15% MoM to NT$947m, or 2% higher than our 
estimates, overall in line. From a MoM perspective, Consumer Electronics & 
Components delivered significant growth MoM, benefitting from the pull-in 
momentum, followed by Cloud and Networking & Computing which achieved strong 
growth MoM. From a YoY perspective, all segments across Consumer Electronics, 
Components, Cloud and Networking & Computing delivered strong growth YoY, and 
management attributed this to the strong pull-in momentum and higher selling prices of 
Consumer Electronics and Computing products. We expect 3Q26E revenues to be +48% 
YoY / +22% QoQ to NT$3.1 trillion. June/ July delivered strong YoY (+52%/ 54% YoY), 
and we expect Aug revenues to remain strong at 73% YoY / 11% MoM to NT$1,051bn, 
supported by AI rack server ramp-up, and strong seasonality of the consumer 
electronics business. Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on a 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

### Page 4

Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI server in strong ramp up; PC to decline in 2H26 on high memory cost

Quanta’s Jul revenue was down 5% QoQ / up 131% YoY, or 21% higher than our 
estimate, which we attribute to the strong rack-level AI server shipments, along with the 
stronger than expected PC shipments at 4.5m units. Looking ahead, we expect 
sequential revenue decline in Aug-Sep, mainly on the memory price hike weighing on PC 
end demand. We model sequential revenue increases in Oct-Dec, supported by the next 
generation rack-level AI server shipments ramp up, which carries a higher ASP . Overall, 
we model Quanta’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 85% / 47% YoY, riding on the 
growing AI infrastructure trend. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral-rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$299 is based on an 
11x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

### Page 6

AVC (3017.TW, Buy): Liquid cooling component ASP and GM upside on the rising complexity of server 
racks; July in line

AVC’s Jul revenue was up 57% YoY / 6% QoQ to NT$19bn, largely in line with our 
estimate. We attribute the strong YoY growth to the strong shipments of AI server liquid 
cooling components, riding on the increasing AI server end demand and a growing liquid 
cooling penetration rate. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
(1) rising shipments of next-generation rack-level AI servers, (2) an increase in liquid 
cooling dollar content in next-generation AI server racks given their growing complexity, 
and (3) a rising liquid cooling penetration rate in both ASIC and GPU AI servers. Maintain 
Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$3,986 on a target P/E multiple of 28.2x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

### Page 7

Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): July revenues sequentially increase; Capex strong increase shows strong AI 
demand

Wiwynn July revenues were in line with our estimates at NT$118bn, driven by solid 
general servers growth and its major ASIC customers’ new generation of ASIC AI servers 
(air-cooling) entering mass production since June 2026. Looking ahead, we model 
sequential revenues decline in Aug and Sep, considering the high-base of general 
servers, while liquid-cooled ASIC AI servers of its major customer, and GPU AI server 
racks ramp up is in 4Q26E. Nevertheless, the 3Q26 revenues growth would be much 
stronger than 1Q26 (-5% QoQ), and 2Q26 (+1% QoQ), up to +22% QoQ, and we expect 
further acceleration to +32% QoQ in 4Q26, driven by AI servers new models ramp up, AI 
servers expansion from ASIC to GPU, potential ASIC AI servers SKU expansion, and less 
impact of business model transition from Buy & Sell to Consign for general servers. 
Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$3,352.55. Our target price is 
based on 20x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.

### Page 8

Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): July and 2Q26 beat; AI server rack in a strong ramp-up with customer 
expansion and specification upgrades

Wistron’s July revenue was up 61% YoY to NT$308bn, or 30% higher than our estimate, 
reflecting strong AI server rack demand from neo-cloud and enterprises, offsetting the 
impact of the model transition. We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through 
Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26, which 
will drive up the ASP , along with strong AI spending and the expansion of company’s 
customer base. The new GPU AI server racks have various racks across GPU, CPU, and 
LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming quarters. On PC, the company’s July shipments 
were at 1.6m units (-20% MoM / YoY), reflecting modest demand under high memory 
costs. Despite PC shipments likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in 
since late 2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, 
driving 2H26 revenue up +28% HoH. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wistron 
Our 12-month target price of NT$281 is based on a 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived 
from PC/server peer EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a 
major factor for the stock’s share price performance.   
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected PC market recovery, 2) 
slower-than-expected AI server demand ramp-up, and 3) weaker-than-expected 
demand on general servers.

### Page 9

Gigabyte (2376.TW, Neutral): AI server in expansion; Strong capex commitment to capture the 
demand; July and 2Q26 beat

Gigabyte’s Jul revenue was up 60% YoY / 3% MoM, or 18% higher than our estimate, 
which we attribute to the strong shipments performance of both baseboard and 
rack-level AI servers. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Sep, supported by the 
rising AI server shipments, and we expect revenues to stay high in 3Q / 4Q26E post 
strong growth in 1H26, driving 2H26 revenues growth at +15% HoH. Management 
remains positive on 2H26 growth driven by strong AI demand, customers’ faster AI 
deployment, and Gigabyte’s expanding customer base and enhancing services. 
Management expects their customers’ infrastructure readiness and components 
procurement to continue to improve in 2H26, supporting the procurement on AI servers. 
Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte 
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$487. Our TP is 
based on a 14.7x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

### Page 10

ASUS (2357.TW, Neutral): PC enjoying product mix upgrade; AI server client base expansion; July & 
2Q26 beat

ASUS’s Jul revenue was up 53% YoY / down 21% QoQ to NT$84bn, or 16% higher than 
our estimate, reflecting strong AI servers demand, along with PC product mix upgrade. 
Looking forward, we expect sequential revenue growth in Aug-Sep, considering the low 
base in Jul, followed by sequential revenue declines in Oct-Nov, as the rising memory 
cost would weigh PC end demand and AI servers model transition. Overall, our estimate 
shows double-digit YoY revenue growth for 3Q / 4Q26E, supported by PC product mix 
upgrade and rack-level AI server ramp up. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Asus 
We are Neutral-rated on ASUS with a 12-month target price of NT$1,109. Our target 
price is based on 15.7x 2027E P/E, which is derived from brand makers’ average ratio of 
2027E P/E to 2028E NI YoY and OPM. We see EPS growth and OPM as major factors for 
the stock’s share performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger / weaker-than-expected PC market 
growth; 2) faster / slower-than-expected AI and gaming PC growth; and 3) faster / 
slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

### Page 11

Compal (2324.TW, Neutral): AI server capacity in expansion; July better than expected

Compal’s Jul revenue was up 38% YoY / down 15% MoM to NT$81bn, or 10% higher 
than our estimate, reflecting AI servers shipment ramp up. Jul PC shipments was down 
22% YoY / 25% MoM to 1.8m units, reflecting the impact of 1H26 demand pull-in amid 
the rising memory cost. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
the company’s rising AI server shipments, AI server capacity expansion, and PC product 
mix upgrade towards high end model (e.g. AI PC). Overall, we expect Compal’s 3Q / 
4Q26E revenues to grow at 6% / 10% QoQ, benefiting from the rising AI server end 
demand from Neocloud clients. We remain positive on Compal’s business diversification, 
supported by global capacity expansion in the US, Taiwan, and Vietnam, while consumer 
electronics still accounts for the majority of the company’s total revenue, which demand 
could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain Neutral on 
fair valuation.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$41.3. Our target 
price is based on 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM 
companies as major factors for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

Inventec (2356.TW, Neutral): AI server supporting growth ahead; Memory cost weighing on PC

Inventec’s Jul revenue was up 66% YoY / down 12% MoM, or 32% higher than our 
estimate, which we attribute to the strong AI server end demand, while the MoM decline 
was mainly due to a decline in PC shipments, given the demand pull-in in 1H26. We 
expect sequential revenue decline in Aug-Sep, mainly on a decline in PC shipments from 
a consumption pull in amid rising memory costs. Meanwhile, growth momentum should 
recover in Oct-Dec, supported by AI server shipment ramp up. Overall, we expect the 
company to deliver double-digit YoY revenue growth in 3Q / 4Q26E, on rising AI server 
end demand and Inventec’s capacity expansion. We remain positive on Inventec’s AI 
server business expansion, while the rising memory cost would cap the PC shipments 
growth ahead. Maintain Neutral.

### Page 14

Pegatron (4938.TW, Sell): AI server driving 2H26 growth; PC growth capped by rising memory cost; Sell

Pegatron’s Jul revenue was +12% YoY / -6% MoM to NT$86bn, and we attribute the 
MoM decline to the sluggish PC end demand post the demand pull-in in 1H26, causing 
Pegatron’s PC shipments to decline to 0.40m units in Jul from 0.65m units in Jun. We 
model double digit MoM revenue growth in Aug-Sep, considering the low base in Jul, 
followed by modest sequential growth in Oct to Dec, mainly driven by the strong AI 
server shipments growth. Management remains positive on the momentum of AI server 
business in 2H26E, supported by the strong end demand from Neocloud and CSP clients. 
Overall, we expect Pegatron’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at +20% / +27% YoY. We are 
positive on Pegatron’s AI server business expansion, while we expect the tight memory 
supply to cap the consumer electronics growth. Maintain Sell.

Earnings revision: Pegatron’s 2Q26 revenue, GM, and Opex ratio are largely in line with 
our estimate, while 2Q26 non-op income is higher than expected, leading 2Q26 net 
income 22% higher than our estimate. We factor in Pegatron’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 
result and raise our 2026-28E net incomes by 10% / 8% / 7%, mainly on higher revenues 
and lower opex ratios. We raise our 2026-28E revenues by 5% / 5% / 4%, mainly on 
higher contribution from AI server business. Our 2026-28E opex ratios are slightly 
revised up, mainly on higher R&D expense to support the AI server specification 
upgrades and PC product mix upgrade towards AI PC.

Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

For the month of September 2026, we estimate AirTAC’s monthly revenue will increase 
to Rmb827mn (+6% MoM, +17% YoY)/NT$3,930mn (+6% MoM, +27% YoY) 
We expect September revenue will go up by 6% MoM to Rmb827mn/NT$3,930mn, 
mainly driven by the improvement in daily pneumatic revenue (Rmb30.9mn in Sep vs. 
Rmb 29.1mn in Aug) despite the same number of working days. The improvement in 
daily pneumatic revenue is driven by the strong growth from key customers, particularly 
in electronics (we also expect peers to see strong demand in electronics, see here), 
followed by batteries and machine tools, and we expect demand to improve following 
the inventory pull-in after the Europe summer holiday.

Investment view: Strong YoY momentum in electronics could drive overall growth. 
Maintain positive view on AirTAC 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AirTAC, as we continue to see key end-segments 
performance tracking ahead of the company’s 2026 full year guidance of 20%+ YoY

### Page 3

growth (see here), with electronics/battery/machine tools delivering 20%/64%/31% 
YTD YoY growth while total revenue in Rmb is tracking at 25% YTD YoY growth. The 
strong YTD performance is in line with our expectation that AirTAC is a key beneficiary 
of the factory automation/industrial automation localization trend in mainland China, as 
well as benefiting from the prolonged upcycle supported by government stimulus 
policies. However, we maintain caution on the potential of exaggerated orders (including 
advance or duplicate orders), and will continue to monitor the underlying end-demand 
closely. 
Furthermore, we believe the company is well-positioned to continue to gain market 
share in the China pneumatic market, particular given its pricing strategy, (1) its 
pneumatic products pricing remain competitively priced at 10%+ below international 
peers, and (2) the company has maintained stable pricing in China (China accounts for 
~95% of total revenue in 2026E per GSe) amid the inflationary cost environment, while 
the company will continue to enhance internal operational efficiency to mitigate the 
rising cost risk (see here). As a result, we continue to see backlog accumulating and 
expect the company to maintain a high UTR to digest these orders. 
Maintain Buy on AirTAC with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,925. 
Investment Thesis - AirTAC 
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 28% market share 
in 2025, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality 
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast 
its market share will reach 30% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner 
in a deflationary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC 
continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing 
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 30-35% OPM) and 
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure 
and financial health: with best-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring 
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to 
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring. 
Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long term given its 
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the 
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to 
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E 
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC. 
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,925 is based on a target P/E of 26.0x on 
our 2027E EPS (0.25x STDV above avg of the last pneumatic upcycle). 
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected 
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in 
mainland China.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
On the latest earnings call, Broadcom's CEO noted that the company is building
substrate capacity in its Singapore factory with a partner. We believe he was
referring to AST, the JV Broadcom has been developing with Toppan (7911.T, Not
Covered) since March 2024 (news). This is Toppan's first overseas substrate plant,
with phase 1 of the two-phase fab targeted to start production by EoY 2026. We see

Broadcom securing FC-BGA substrate supply rather than bringing production in-
house, and therefore expect limited impact on the current supply positions of
Unimicron and NYPCB.
On BT substrate pricing, the price hikes since 2H25 have been driven by raw material
cost inflation, including T glass, gold and copper. We have expected T glass supply
constraints to peak in 2026 and gradually ease from 2027 as more suppliers are
qualified. Thus, it would not surprise us if raw-material-driven price hikes plateau

going forward. That said, our OW thesis on Unimicron and NYPCB centers on ABF
substrate tightness, so we see limited impact from BT price fluctuations on our
ratings for both stocks.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
NYPCB's share price was down 12% in the past two trading days (vs. TAIEX up
0.8%).
Investors have asked us if NYPCB would face share losses if Broadcom (AVGO,
covered by Joe Moore) were to produce substrates on its own.
Separately, we have been receiving questions from investors about the pricing
outlook for BT substrate in 4Q.

We see limited impact on our OW thesis for Unimicron and NYPCB, both of which
produce BT substrates and are Broadcom's incumbent suppliers.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate

value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.

CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 079: 260906_ms_semicon-TW

### Page 1

Ethan Jia
Research Associate
Ethan.Jia@morganstanley.com                                                +852 3963-2287
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
AI semi capex spending in Taiwan remains strong into 2027 to
solve the supply chain bottleneck. Keep OW on TSMC,
MediaTek, Hon Precision, Allring, MPI, Winway, etc.
Titans of Taiwan AI semi supply chain - key message from the  CEO Forum
fireside chat  –  Taiwan is still one of the bottlenecks of AI infrastructure buildout.

On the stage, TSMC Deputy Co-COO Mr. Hou  said that he had never seen demand
growing and changing this frequently as it has over the past six months, while the
tool delivery from WFE partners are still not enough.  MediaTek CEO Mr. Tsai was
asking for more capacity support from ASE, TSMC, and Unimicron.
Why investors came to this trade show in Taiwan: We believe the tech industry
and capital markets are finding ways to keep AI hardware spending sustainable in

coming years. With memory becoming the major bottleneck and cost item for AI
capex, investors are checking whether technologies can improve AI computing
efficiency, for example, CPO to replace copper connectors in XPU scale-up. All in all,
the goal is to reduce per-token cost.  Please see following section for details.
Key focus areas during  SEMICON Taiwan:
1. Silicon photonics for AI networking and  testing process development : In
our previous report, AI Supply Chain: GPU Architecture Optimization Given

Memory Shortage and Supernode Challenge, we indicated that future GPU/
ASIC scale-up would depend on CPO/NPO (“inter-rack CPO connectivity
remains Nvidia's preferred direction”). For example, the NV576 Rubin Ultra in
2027 may use CPO switch trays to connect eight NVL72 server racks. Silicon
Photonics and PIC (Photonic integrated circuit), lasers, CPO, and related
testing present large investment opportunities.
2. Memory optimization from high cost : Instead of adding density, increasing

bandwidth and fully utilizing existing memory resources has become the key
technology trend. Our recent supply chain checks suggest that Rubin Ultra
CPU is highly likely to use 8-Hi HBM4, around one-third fewer than 12-Hi in
Rubin. But by increasing HBM4 base-die units and migrating to HBM4e, plus
some software optimization, total memory bandwidth should be sufficient
for a token output increase.
3. "More than Moore"- Panel-level packaging and Intel EMIB-T - We visited the

Toray (Toray Industries covered by Takato Watabe) booth to check EMIB-T
substrate manufacturing yield, because the company supplies the TCB
bonder.  We expect Intel to provide technical support, and expect MediaTek’s
TPU v9 (HumuFish) to get sufficient EMIB-T substrate supply in 2028.
4. Although it wasn’t the main focus of the SEMICON , we think TSMC's
Moore’s Law migration (for energy efficiency) and MediaTek's ASIC enabling
(to save on chip budgets) are  keys to make AI capex more sustainable.

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
September 6, 2026 05:24 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Need for more capacity to support strong AI semi demand
into 2027
Titans of Taiwan AI semi supply chain - CEO Forum
The discussion focused on how strong AI demand is leading to ongoing undersupply from
the whole semi industry . We believe AI demand remains strong and is able to support
strong revenue growth for ASE, TSMC, and MediaTek in coming years. At the same time,
Taiwan's semi supply chain is also expanding capex and opex aggressively to catch up to

the demand.
Stock ideas around SEMICON Taiwan:We remain OW on TSMC given its dominance in
leading-edge foundry. MediaTek remains our Top Pick given its competitive ASIC design
service. We hosted a booth tour to visit Hon Precision, AllRing, MPI, and WinWay to cross-
check CoWoS and CoPoS expansion plans, as well as optical insertion technology. We are
OW on all the above stocks.
Key focus areas during SEMICON Taiwan 2026
1. Silicon Photonics for AI networking and the testing process development:

Please also see Semiconductor Production Equipment: SEMICON Taiwan Day 1 –
Takeaways from SiPh Seminar from Suzune Tamura. We share the view with Tamura-san
that testing is the key challenge for CPO  mass production. During SEMICON Taiwan,
several companies showcased their solutions or components  for CPO testing. Although
the solutions might be different, all companies were providing similar information: that
CPO testing is still not clearly defined while most of the companies are still trying to get

qualified by major customers with their equipment/solutions. The speed and precision of
optical alignment remains the key to throughput, and  we believe there is still room for
improvement.

3. Panel-level packaging, Intel EMIB-T, and TSMC 3.5D CoWoS: We visited the Toray

manufacturing yield, because the company supplies the TCB bonder. Our finding suggests
that tool accuracy and supply are not the major issues; rather, it is substrate customers’

know-how in aligning silicon-bridge bonding to PCB. We expect Intel to provide technical
support, and expect MediaTek’s TPU v9 (HumuFish) to get sufficient EMIB-T substrate
supply in 2028. For 3D IC packaging, please also see Semiconductor Production
Equipment: SEMICON Taiwan Day 2 - Heterogeneous Integration Seminar Takeaways.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

ab 6 September 2026
Global Research
Largan Precision
Strong CPO ramp priced ahead of qualification
The re-rating has outpaced qualification progress; downgrade from Buy to Sell
Largan shares have risen 242% since April on optimism around its CPO FAU progress.
While we stay constructive on Largan's CPO prospects, we believe the market is pricing
in confidence ahead of still-early qualification progress, particularly given a crowded
competitive field. Largan only starts its sample line late Q3, integrating its Prism Micro

Lens Array and Fiber Array with AOET's V-groove and Corning's fiber. We expect  several
quarters to improve yields, gain integrator acceptance (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and
convert its initial production-oriented FA program into volume while competing against
incumbent TFC and vertically integrated rivals incl. Coherent. We downgrade our rating
from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-
18x range well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 PT based on 15x smartphone lens

+ 50x CPO base case for 40% share  (upper end of optical peers  at 14-72x).
Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low) and
1 FA design in an FAU. A risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028

and also competes with incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and
other FAU vendors like Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not
requiring Largan's initial V-groove approach. Largan's push toward a complete solution
may also conflict with integration partners.
Core smartphone lens tracking behind amid memory headwinds
We also see risk on near-term results with Q326 sales tracking light. Aug sales were NT
$5bn, +3% MoM, -16% YoY, taking QTD revenue to NT$10.1bn, 53% of UBSe/Street

Q326. Sept would need to grow +73% MoM to meet UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ vs.
normal seasonal +8% MoM. We expect flattish builds at best for its flagship customer
(80% of sales) needing to raise price to pass on memory costs +US$200-300 YoY albeit
some support from high-end introducing variable-aperture lens, while foldable lens
specs remain similar. Factoring muted smartphone growth but healthy margins, we view
a mid-teens multiple appropriate for this business, in line with its historical range.

Stock prices in our upside case ahead of traction; risk-reward skews negative
We maintain basic '26E/27E EPS at NT$190/NT$214 and NT$5,000 SOTP PT based on 1)
15x smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730
(40% share, 100k switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of
peers due to its premium ~40-45% OpM. We view risk/reward skewed negative with
downside to NT$2,500 on limited CPO success and softer smartphones, base case NT

$5,000 for CPO traction, and upside 22% if the stock is bid to our upside case NT$9,000
based on top-end 70x P/E on CPO earnings at 60% share of a higher US$1.58bn TAM.

### Page 2

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 2
Largan Precision UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Largan's CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
We stay constructive but the company still has hurdles to clear in a crowded space. Its scale and track
record in high-end smartphone lens gives it an opportunity supplying key components of the CPO

FAU assembly, traditional strength in precision lens micro assemblies, alignment accuracy, in-house
tooling and automation that could give it a differentiated solution. Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan
suggests the company is still early in qualification, with its sample production line for customer
evaluation readied in late September and still requiring work with a system integrator and
qualification with the fabless customer and ODMs.  We still see a year to move from sampling to

design-in but if successful timed to meet expected market acceleration in 2028-29.
Q: Will there be further technology upgrades driving the lens cycle in the coming years?
We expect spec upgrades for Largan's core lens business to come in H226 from variable aperture
shipments for the iPhone 18 (which Genius does not currently participate though may enter over
time, as seen with periscope lenses). Key offset though is weaker sales with Q3 tracking light and

suggesting a softer build due to memory headwinds. Further upside could come from broader
adoption of 48MP modules in future iPhone models. Looking longer term, Apple is reportedly
exploring 200MP periscope lens and adjacent opportunities to penetrate physical AI categories with
precision lens including robotics, autonomous systems and AR/VR devices.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell as we view the market expectations as now
pricing in a bullish scenario for strong market traction with the company still at an early stage of

qualification, still needing to improve its process and yields, and competing in a crowded space also
against an existing incumbent. We expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain
acceptance from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI GPU
fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and other rivals. We downgrade
Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x

range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens +
50x CPO base case for40% share  (upper-end of optical peer range of 14-72x).
Largan has demonstrated its samples with partners at Computex and Semicon Taiwan and cited its
goals to ramp CPO as its next opportunity. The company is still early in qualifications with a sample
automated line in the coming months followed by design-in engagement with system integrators,

ODMs and the fabless customer to target design-in on 3rd gen CPO solutions in 2028. For
smartphone lens, its top customer had a strong iPhone 17 cycle although acknowledged it would
have to raise price to pass on higher memory costs which could dampen growth. Sales QTD are
tracking slightly light as Largan also competes with 2nd source Genius. Our supply chain checks and
management commentary points to introduction of higher value variable aperture lens in H226.

Largan is currently trading above the high end of its historical range at 39x/35x our 2026E/27E EPS vs.
a long-term range of 13-18x forward consensus earning, pricing in success penetrating the CPO
component market ahead of the earnings stream which would only start late in 2027 into 2028. We
note optical component peers trade at 14-72x with an average of ~38x 2027E P/E (Genius at 25x
2027 P/E). We maintain our SOTP valuation with our price target at NT$5000 based on 1) 15x

smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730 (40% share, 100k
switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of peers due to its premium ~40-
45% OpM. We view the stock at NT$7,400 now pricing in CPO traction on a 50x multiple of our
upside NT$76 case based on 60% share of an upside US$1.6bn opportunity.

### Page 3

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 3
Largan Precision UBS Research
commentary
Largan shares have risen 242% since 1 April on optimism around its CPO fiber array unit
(FAU) progress. While we stay constructive post our feedback at Semicon Taiwan on
Largan's ability to develop CPO solutions, we now believe the market is pricing in a level
of confidence that has run well ahead of qualification progress which is still early and
competing in a crowded field. Largan will only start its sample line in late Q3 integrating

its Prism Micro Lens Array and Fiber Array, AOET's V-groove and Corning's glass fiber.
From there, we expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain acceptance
from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI
GPU fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and rivals including
Coherent with own lens/FAU. The company did note on its Q226 call that it had secured
one mass production commitment for its fiber array to an FAU maker that could ramp by

H227 although early to conclude the magnitude of this engagement or end customer
design-in, with the other customers still at proof-of-concept phase.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and
the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT
$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens + 50x CPO base case for 40% share
(upper-end of optical component peers trading at a  range of 14-72x).

Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low). A
risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028 and also competes with

incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and other FAU vendors like
Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not requiring Largan's initial V-
groove approach. Largan's business model may also conflict with integration partners as
it also attempts to build toward a complete solution.
We estimate Largan's Total Addressable Market in a downside case is US$270mn if it
only supplies sub-components into a market that continues to push out CPO due to

reliability concerns relative to pluggables and NPO/XPO options, US$1.08bn in a base
case with 100k switches and 72 FAUs per switch at US$150 ASP , and US$1.6bn in an
upside case with 125k switches, 72 FAUs per switch and US$175 ASP .  For Largan, we
estimate downside case 10% share yields NT$810mn sales and NT$2.20 EPS, base case
40% share, NT$13.0bn sales and NT$35 EPS, and upside case 60% share, NT$28.4bn
sales and NT$76 EPS.
Although we are optimistic on the market opportunity scaling up and Largan's

competencies and progress developing these solutions, Largan's traction is not yet
certain and it will compete in a crowded landscape. We believe Himax is already
supplying wafer level optics for the collimator function with FOCI (3363.TWO) and TFC
(300394.SZ) already has demonstrated capability and design-in on the FAU and lens
assembly. Others that could vertically integrate include Coherent, Senko, Corning and
Sumitomo, and we also note Genius sending samples and Sunny Optical's intention to

develop for this market.

Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan indicates Coherent can make the FAU and lens by itself
through capabilities merging with II-VI and demonstrated all in-house components at
the booth except for the metal transceiver casing.

Smartphone lens business tracking behind, should have a mild 2026-27
Largan reported August sales at NT$5bn,   is below seasonal 63-67% at only 53% of
Q326 UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ and down 16% YoY, suggesting muted momentum
into its flagship customer (~80% of sales). July EPS was NT$13.94, 27% of UBSe Q3 NT
$51.52 suggesting in-line GM/OpM at ~50%/39% but the lighter sales are more of a

concern.
UBS expects flattish builds at best and a mild spec upgrade with one model variable
aperture but foldable at similar lens spec and higher memory cost (+US$200-300 YoY).
We still see mild growth and mid-teens multiple acceptable for the smartphone
business, similar to its long-term average.

UPSIDE (NT$9,000): Our upside valuation of NT$3,655 is based on  its average P/E of 16x
off our 2027 upside case EPS of NT$235. We factor in faster than expected

trading at 14-72x 2027E P/E to an implied valuation of NT$5,345, and  total share price at
NT$9,000.
BASE (NT$5,000):  Our price target for its smartphone business is NT$3,270, with a 15x
2027 P/E applied on its base case EPS of NT$214 slightly below the long-term average of

its historical P/E band to reflect the macro uncertainty. Paired with our base scenario of its
CPO business at NT$35 EPS and 50x P/E, we arrive at an implied valuation of NT$1,730,
and Largan's aggregate PT  at NT$5,000.
DOWNSIDE (NT$2,500):   Downside valuation at NT$2,392, based on low-end of its
range 13x 2027 P/E. We take into consideration slower adoption of FAUs at its downside
EPS of NT$2 and the same 50x P/E to  an implied valuation of NT$108 for

CPO contribution, and Largan's total share price at NT$2,500.

Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation -32.4%
Forecast dividend yield 1.1%
Forecast stock return -31.4%
Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return -37.6%
Company Description
Founded in 1987, Largan Precision (Largan) is one of the world's leading manufacturers of
lenses used in cameras and IT products. It specialises in plastic/hybrid lenses and, to a lesser
extent, glass lenses and modules. In 2022, 20MP+ camera lenses were 10-20% of Largan's

shipment mix, 10MP lenses were 50-60%, 8MP lenses were 0-10% and other lenses were
30-40%.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business.
We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from smartphone OEM
clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a slower-than-
expected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success below the

market's elevated expectations.
We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-expected CPO qualification process at Largan.
In addition, if the company demonstrates exceptionally efficient and highly accurate
automation capabilities, it could support greater market share gains within the industry than
we currently anticipate.

the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

Hiwin Technologies Corp.  | Asia Pacific
August Sales +1% MoM/+42%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Hiwin's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales were NT$2,708mn (+1% MoM/+42% YoY).
• QTD revenue was 67% of our 3Q26 estimate, at NT$7,996mn (+6% QoQ/
+33% YoY), and 68% of consensus, at NT$7,884mn (+5% QoQ/+32% YoY).
Our view:
• We think 3Q26 is tracking slightly ahead of expectations.

• Looking into 2H26, we maintain the view that Hiwin should continue to see
revenue growth amid the industry recovery and margin expansions driven by
higher utilization/price hikes.
• We stay constructive on the stock, with current valuation at 29x 2027e P/E
vs. a peak cycle level of 35-40x.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple
of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.

n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
